## Analyzer Architecture Summary

kueue is a Kubernetes component. managing 2 CRD(s)\: ClusterQueue, LocalQueue. deploying 11 workload(s) (controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager) with 11 container(s). watching 14 resource type(s). exposing 2 service(s) on ports\: 443. Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 8080. with 41 webhook(s) (21 validating, 20 mutating). defining 11 API spec type(s) across 89 struct(s) with 284 total fields. with 635 operator config constants. following 11 reconciliation steps across 4 controller(s). exposing 33 Prometheus metrics. reporting 3 status condition types. Python k8s client with 4 API call(s) and 1 CRD kind reference(s). integrates with Kueue, management relationship via labels/annotations. deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization. kustomize overlays\: config/alpha-enabled, config/components/certmanager, config/components/crd, config/components/internalcert, config/components/kueue-viz, config/components/manager, config/components/prometheus, config/components/rbac, config/components/visibility, config/components/visibility-apf, config/components/webhook, config/default, config/dev, config/kueue-viz, config/prometheus, config/rhoai, config/visibility-apf. mounts 1 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: manager-config.
